khubi shundor ekti natok. khubi shundor bhabe ekjon mayer koshto o mon-mansiktar obostha tule dhora hoyeche. shei sathe aro shundor bhabe futiye tola hoyeche poribarer baki sodosjder Bichar bibekhin acoron, prdhanot purushotber dohai deya puroshotbhin sbamider acoron. tobe jodi shami songsar calate jotheshto skhoom hon, tahole bacca-songsar rekhe cakri na korai valo. ekjon ma cakri koruk ba na i koruk, odhikangosh sbamider acoron emon osohjogitamulok i hoye thake, songsarer shobai to notun ma howa mayeder pichone lege thakei, tar sathe shami namok manushtike nanan ojacit kotha bole tader kanke bishiye tole. tokhon sbamirao tader notun ma howa buyer pashe thaken na. eta sotjii du:okhojonok. ekta songsar kokhnoi karo ekar kontribiushane sukhi hoy na, jokhni shami tar strir kaje sohjogita kore, sokol prtikultake pash katiye strike somorthon diye pashe thake, tokhni ekti songsar sukher hoy. dhonjbad janai poricalok raj vaike, ei natokti nirman kore somaje ekti mesej deoyar jonjo.
